HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B001","root_001630/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1be8786f9a73b821f664","text":"1. **Facial softness is the output of a complete ease-producing system.** **Lexical:** `و ج ه` B001, the visible face/front; `ن ع م` B001, pleasant well-being and bestowed favor; and B002, softness and easeful living. **Cue:** this face reverses the labor, weariness, fire, failed food, and hunger of 88:3-7, then unfolds into satisfaction, a high garden, quiet, flowing water, and raised couches in 88:9-13. **Role:** the face is the public indicator, while the two `ن ع م` branches combine material ease and its visible softness. **Abduction:** I infer that the later habitat causes or sustains the facial condition; the packet supplies ordered elaboration rather than an explicit cause. **Reading change:** `ناعمة` reads as embodied infrastructure, not merely a pleasant expression.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_213e948cb5d3fdf63d85","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:8:3\",\"root_001525\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rahatlık, kişi ile yerin birbirine uygun düşmesi anlamına da gelir; tekrarlanan yer bildiren ifadeler, bedeni yalnız çevrelenmiş değil, ortamıyla uyumlu gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_304c19b5a8ab20008d40","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:8:3\",\"root_001525\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Rahatlığı anlatan sözün otlayan hayvanları da çağrıştırması, yüzleri daha önce bozulan su, otlak, süt ve dolgunluk zincirinin onarılmış bolluğuna bağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001630/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_388ad0264b0b6d11af9f","text":"2. **The face also displays restored standing.** **Lexical:** `و ج ه` B006, \"status and eminence,\" activated with `ن ع م` B001. **Cue:** the face is followed by `عَالِيَةٍ` in 88:10 and `مَّرْفُوعَةٌ` in 88:13, while the preceding group was lowered and exhausted. **Role:** the status branch translates vertical surroundings into social-personal elevation visible at the face. **Abduction:** I infer that spatial height figures restored rank; no social title is stated. **Reading change:** the plural faces are not anonymous surfaces only; they are public persons whose standing has been elevated along with their setting.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B001","root_001630/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3ae72a8e0f7124b25b29","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:8:1\",\"root_001630\",[\"B006\"]],[\"88:8:3\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yüzün itibarı da gösterebilmesi, sonraki yüksekliklerle birleşerek önceki alçaltılmış grubun karşısında yeniden kazanılmış bir konumu dışarıdan görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001630/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_454d4eb45fcdabc9322b","text":"88:9-13 decomposed facial softness into a mechanism: accepted endeavor, a fitting high place, quiet, flow, and supported rest. The exact vertical culmination at 88:13 especially sharpened `و ج ه` B006, making restored standing visible in the face rather than leaving `ناعمة` purely tactile.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002","root_001630/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_61aa0236a1d1f3cee427","text":"1. **Faces softened by ease after the low-face contrast.** Evidence: `و ج ه` B001 face/front; `ن ع م` B002 softness and easeful living, B001 pleasant well-being/favor. Structural cue: this repeats the `وجوه يومئذ` frame from 88:2 with the opposite predicate. Role: the face again becomes the outward display, now of favor and ease. Abductive move: the day makes inner condition visible on the front of the body. Reading change: the ayah reads as the visual surface of bestowed ease.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_62008bbbb8390c4dda76","text":"3. **Place that agrees with its inhabitant.** Evidence: `ن ع م` B011 a place agreeing with someone; later `ج نن` B003 garden, `ع ل و` B001 high place. Structural cue: 88:8 immediately leads to satisfaction with effort and residence in a high garden. Role: the face’s ease anticipates a fitting environment. Abductive move: visible well-being results when person, effort, and place are in agreement. Reading change: `ناعمة` reads as environmental congruence, not only bodily softness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_995d2583fc4f85612b13","text":"3. **Ease means that the place and its inhabitant fit one another.** **Lexical:** `ن ع م` B011, \"a place agreeing with someone.\" **Cue:** after `ناعمة`, the locative `فِى`/`فِيهَا` sequence repeatedly nests the subject within garden, silence, spring, and raised resting places in 88:10-13. **Role:** B011 assigns the environment the role of agreeing with the body rather than merely surrounding it. **Abduction:** I infer reciprocal fit from the repeated locatives and the satisfied state in 88:9. **Reading change:** the face's softness registers successful habitation: person and place have become mutually suitable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B001","root_001525/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c4598ec2d67c54a3ab7d","text":"2. **Livestock/camel activation inside blessing.** Evidence: `ن ع م` B005 grazing livestock, especially camels; later `ء ب ل` B001 camels; earlier `ض ر ع`, `س م ن`, `ج و ع` pastoral deprivation. Structural cue: the root that marks pleasant favor also names livestock, and camels later become the first created sign. Role: `ناعمة` can carry prosperous pastoral fullness in contrast to the failed food chain. Abductive move: in this packet, blessing is intelligible through well-watered, well-fed animal life as well as human comfort. Reading change: the happy faces read as nourished and provisioned, not merely emotionally pleased.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ef64b9d5869f8565bfda","text":"The later garden furnishings made `ناعمة` spatial: ease is distributed across couches, cups, cushions, and carpets. The camel root also pulled `ن ع م` toward livestock blessing, so the faces are comforted within a broader provision system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B001","root_001525/B002","root_001630/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f68db2a5eb82fe32c7e7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:8:1\",\"root_001630\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:8:3\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yüzlerdeki rahatlık, yalnız bir ifade değil; memnuniyet, bahçe, sessizlik, su ve dinlenme yerlerinden oluşan bütün bir kolaylık düzeninin bedende görünen sonucudur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
